    The shape of the residue is one finding. Nine of the fourteen are the same defect: the rule exists, the compiler is right, and the page a first contact actually reads does not carry it. docs/README.md promises reference/ is "the single answer layer" and routes spec/ for "the exact rule for an edge case"; four shipped language features and two builtin overloads live only on the far side of that split. That is a documentation-architecture question, and it is the thing two independent strangers hit hardest on the same afternoon.

    Two procedure defects found the same way, and they belong here because they cost future rounds rather than this one:

    • The setup strip deletes docs/internals, docs/rfc and tests, so every probe will report the links into them as dead. The handle round filed six that are not. The deletion list should say so.
    • pi-run.log records only a failed launch (Model "mimo-v2.5" is ambiguous across providers), so which model wrote either report is not recorded anywhere. The procedure has a whole section on choosing a model and no step that writes down the one used.

    129. Every user's build drew two -Wunused-value warnings out of core:strings — FIXED 2026-09-24

    Pinned-by: ! ( ./tychoc --emit-c tests/pkg/vendor_deps/main.ty | grep '= h_strings___slice_ok(' | grep -qv '((void)0); })' )

    The both row of #128. slice_bytes and slice_str each open with _slice_ok(...) or_return on a Result(void, SliceErr), and src/tychoc.c emitted every or_return as a statement expression ending in _orN.okv — the ok payload — whether or not there was one. On a void result that tail is a placeholder nobody reads, so cc warned about it in every program that imported core:strings, naming a line in a generated file that is deleted after the build. Both probes hit it independently and both spent time establishing the warning was not theirs.

    tychoc1 never had it: _orret in compiler/emit/emit.ty already ends a void payload with ((void)0). The reference compiler now does the same. The pin reads the emitted C for the two call sites rather than grepping the compiler source, and was run against the unfixed compiler first: it goes red there.

    130. A use after close(h) compiled, and is_null could not tell closed from never opened — FIXED 2026-09-24

    Pinned-by: ./tychoc --emit-c -o /dev/null tests/reject/handle_use_after_close.ty 2>&1 | grep -q 'is used after' Pinned-by: ./tychoc1 --typecheck tests/reject/handle_close_twice.ty 2>&1 | grep -q 'is used after' Pinned-by: sh tests/ffi/run.sh Pinned-by: ./tychoc1 --typecheck tests/reject/handle_close_param.ty 2>&1 | grep -q 'is a handle parameter'

    The handle — row of #128. close(h) runs the destructor and nulls the variable, so every later use passed null to C, a second close was silently a no-op, and is_null(h) answered true for "closed" and for "the opener failed" alike. Spec §25 documented use-after-close as permitted. For the one resource kind whose whole point is *exactly once*, that left the remaining misuse undetectable both at compile time and at run time.

    Neither compiler has dataflow, and none was added. The rule leans on two properties handles already have: they are affine and they cannot be reassigned. A close(h) statement therefore closes h for the rest of its block, and blocks nested after it. Every later mention there is on a path through the close, so it is rejected: a call that passes h, a second close(h), and is_null(h), which could only be a constant true. A close inside an if, a loop body or an arm reaches only the end of that block, so a use after it is a maybe and still compiles. The rule claims only certainties, and the maybe case is exercised by tests/ffi/main.ty@use_res_branch_close. src/tychoc.c@shut_mark records the closed variable and resolve_block truncates the record at the block's end. The E_IDENT resolve refuses a closed variable, so shadowing is handled by the ordinary scope lookup. compiler/types/tcheck.ty@_shut_mark does the same in _tblock and in _value_type, whose value-arm statements bypass _tblock. A first cut missed that path and diverged on a close inside a value if arm. The message is word-for-word in both compilers.

    What stays open is the maybe path. There is_null(h) still cannot distinguish a branch that closed from an opener that failed. Answering that would need a second run-time state or a new builtin, and the surface is frozen. The spec says to keep a bool beside the handle.

    A finding made on the way, not fixed here: close(h) on a handle parameter compiles. The callee nulls only its own copy, so the caller's scope-exit free runs the destructor a second time. That is a double free, and it contradicts §25's "the callee does not free it". FIXED 2026-09-24: close(h) on a parameter is now a compile error in both compilers (src/tychoc.c@close_param_check; tychoc1 marks VBind.param where it pushes a parameter). Reproduced first with a destructor that counted its calls: two frees for one open. Function, lambda and generic parameters are all refused; sink/inout handle parameters were already refused at the declaration. Pinned by tests/reject/handle_close_param.ty.

    131. tychoc could not build an inline array reassigned from a call inside a loop — FIXED 2026-09-24

    Pinned-by: ! ( ./tychoc --emit-c tests/inline_arr_reassign_loop.ty | grep -q 'arena_recycle(.*h_[hgb]\.data' ) Pinned-by: make -s grade-check

    Found by tools/tycho-grade/ (#128, layout 4) on its first build: gain = parse4(...) inside the argument loop, with gain a vector[4]f32 read more than once. The reference compiler's in-loop spine recycle (src/tychoc.c@do_recycle) frees a dynamic array's old buffer before the reassignment, and its guard excluded only bounded[N]T from the set of arrays. A vector[N]T and a plain [N]T also store their elements inline, with no .data or .cap, so the emitted recycle was a cc error in the generated file. The guard is now IS_INLINE_ARR, which covers all three. tychoc1 never had it. The pin was run against the unfixed compiler first and goes red there; tests/inline_arr_reassign_loop.ty exercises all three kinds.

    132. math.clamp refused a vector, so a grading kernel's clamp left the vector unit — FIXED 2026-09-24

    Pinned-by: make -s vector-check Pinned-by: make -s grade-check

    #128's layout 6. math.min, math.max and math.clamp are where comparable(T), and a vector is not comparable, so tools/tycho-grade/ did its multiply-add as one vector[4]f32 operation and then clamped four lanes in a scalar loop. The surface is frozen, so the fix is not a new name: the three existing functions accept a vector and are lane-wise. Each lane gives the scalar body's answer, down to the order of clamp's two tests, which decides the result when lo > hi.

    It is a compiler rule and not a math.ty change, because no Tycho body can say "select per lane": if a < b on two vectors has no lane-wise meaning, and surface.lock records the where comparable(T) signature, which stays as it is. Both compilers recognise the three templates by name *and* by their file lying under the corelib root (src/tychoc.c@vlane_kind, compiler/emit/emit.ty@_vmath_of), skip the where and the body walk for them, and emit the body themselves. C has no vector ?: (gcc and clang take it only in C++ and OpenCL), so the body is a lane mask from one vector compare and a blend through a same-size cast. clang -O1 turns a vector[4]f32 clamp into two fcmgt and two bit, with no loop.

    Two things turned up on the way:

    • tychoc1's under_corelib compares directory listings when one side is relative. So a user's math/math.ty "is" corelib/math, and the first cut replaced a user package's own min body. types/load.ty@from_corelib is a textual prefix test, and it is exact for what the loader builds from a core: import. The looser function still drives the unused-local exemption. That is left as found.
    • tychoc1 refuses -3.0 in a vector[4]f32 literal ("element 3 … is the wrong type"), and ./tychoc accepts it. The fixtures here write 0.0 - 2.5. That parity gap is not fixed here.

    133. Inferred-local placeholders collided at a decimal prefix — FIXED 2026-09-26

    Pinned-by: make -s test

    The 20,000-seed compiler fuzzer hit seed 8405: after enough []/None placeholders, tychoc1 emitted invalid C (TychoArrInt1 and a stray numeric suffix). The minimal case is ten inferred empty-array locals followed by a push into each. _fnbody replaced _PT1 before _PT10 (and likewise _PE1 before _PE10), so the shorter token consumed the prefix of the longer. It now substitutes in descending allocation order. tests/emit_many_inferred_arrays.ty pins the ten-placeholder case; the unfixed output was checked to fail C syntax compilation, while tychoc and the fixed tychoc1 both build and print 10.

    134. Generic constructor resolution retained pointers across type-table growth — FIXED 2026-09-26

    Pinned-by: make -s test

    A 17-deep Wrap(Wrap(...(41))) made tychoc SIGSEGV while resolving a valid program. The generic constructor held a StructDef * (and the enum path held EnumDef */Variant *) while recursively resolving payloads. A nested generic constructor could grow and relocate the definition table, invalidating those pointers. The resolver now retains stable table indices and re-reads definitions around recursive calls. tests/generic_ctor_realloc.ty crosses the table-growth boundary for both generic structs and generic enums; both compilers build it and it prints 41 and enum.

    135. tychoc1 left sized and element-wise integer division unguarded — FIXED 2026-09-26

    Pinned-by: make -s test

    The audit's D04/D08 repros were confirmed: division by zero in u32 and in an array lane reached raw C /, which can SIGFPE/SIGILL instead of producing a Tycho diagnostic. The self-hosted emitter now routes signed and unsigned sized integer / and % through the runtime guards, including array, vector and broadcast lanes; the type checker checks literal zero before the element-wise early return. tests/abort/{sized_div_zero,array_div_zero,sized_array_div_zero}.ty and tests/reject/array_div_lit_zero.ty pin the cases. Both compilers now trap with tycho: division by zero for runtime zero and reject the literal divisor.

    136. Empty literals slipped through fixed-array arity checks — FIXED 2026-09-26

    Pinned-by: make -s test

    [] was accepted for [N]T and vector[N]T: the checker had a fixed-count check only on the non-empty literal path. tychoc then emitted an uninitialized inline aggregate; tychoc1 built a zero-length value with a fixed type. The fixed-array count check now includes zero elements, and the self-hosted checker refuses the empty literal in both fixed arrays and vectors. The rejecting twins tests/reject/fixed_array_empty_literal.ty and tests/reject/vector_empty_literal.ty cover both compilers.

    137. Const-sized arrays lost their capacity in the self-hosted checker and emitter — FIXED 2026-09-26

    Pinned-by: make -s test

    _fixsize treated "is a const" as "unknown size", disabling literal arity, push/pop and parameter compatibility checks for [N]T; zero and negative const sizes also escaped. It now resolves the folded const value and enforces a positive length. The emitter separately parsed a vector[N]T count as a raw integer token, turning a valid const into vector width zero; it now resolves same-package consts before registering the vector type. tests/const_sized_arrays.ty pins fixed-array and vector length/element behavior, and five reject fixtures pin arity, push, zero/negative sizes and parameter mismatch.

    138. If/elif chains were nested and quadratically emitted — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: sh tests/elif_chain.sh

    Audit D03/D23 reproduced cubic memory in tychoc1: every nested IfS copied and indented the remaining chain, while the emitter repeatedly concatenated its output. The statement AST now stores a flat IfArm list; typechecking, name resolution, analysis and emission walk each arm once. C output is a linear if/else if chain, assembled from pieces in one pass. A synthetic 10,000-arm chain compiles and runs with both compilers; the generated C stays below 1 MB.

    139. Native C emission nested every elif arm — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: sh tests/elif_chain.sh

    Audit D14 found that tychoc emitted else { if (...) { ... } } at one more brace and indentation level per arm. gen_stmt now walks the existing else-chain iteratively and writes else if at a constant depth. The 10,000-arm case builds and runs with gcc, and the 300-arm case also builds with clang, for both compilers.

    140. Builtin shadowing and self-named initializers miscompiled — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: make -s test

    Audit D09/D10 exposed two emitter mismatches: total := total(...) made the C local hide the function in its own initializer, and tychoc1 dispatched a shadowed builtin such as keys to the user function despite the resolver's warning. Both emitters now evaluate self-naming initializers through a temporary; the self-hosted builtin-dispatch list matches the resolver and C bootstrap. The existing tests/shadow_builtin_selection.ty now pins both builtin precedence and the self-named function call.

    141. Native source loading assumed seekable files — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: sh tests/stream_source.sh

    Audit D11 found that ftell returning -1 for a pipe became a SIZE_MAX-byte fread into an empty allocation. tychoc now reads sources into a growing buffer and checks read errors and size overflow. Both compilers accept source from /dev/stdin and a FIFO; they also refuse /proc/self/cmdline, whose procfs st_size is zero despite nonempty NUL-delimited content. The test is separate from program stdin.

    142. Literal fit checks were skipped by several coercion paths — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: make parse-check; TYCHO_THREADS=8 make -s test

    Audit D06's variadic, UFCS, lambda-return, map-default, channel-send, subscript-store, value-match and f32 literal cases are now checked at the expected destination type. Variadic extras inherit the element type; UFCS resolves the receiver's signature before checking arguments (including chained calls); and the lambda's tail expression uses its declared/expected result type. Both compilers also range-check the scalar broadcast against a sized-array element; the native resolver now calls sized_lit rather than merely tagging the expression. Added ten reject fixtures for the coercion paths and f32 array and vector literals.

    143. Place stores formed stale addresses before an inout RHS could grow the container — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: sh tests/place_store_order.sh

    Audit D01 reproduced both an array write into recycled arena memory and a lost map update: xs[i] = grow(&xs, &i) and m["x"] = grow(&m, 7). The emitters now evaluate non-literal index/key legs before the RHS, materialize the RHS, and form the destination address/slot only after the call returns. Compound map updates snapshot the old value and re-find the slot after RHS growth. The new tests/place_store_order.ty checks array reallocation, map rehash, index mutation during the call, and compound map assignment; both compilers also pass its clang build.

    144. tychoc1 -g emitted unescaped source paths in #line directives — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: sh tests/debug_path.sh

    Audit D12's backslash and quote could corrupt the C #line string; a newline could split the directive itself. Both emitters now escape quotes, backslashes, line breaks, tabs, and other control bytes. tests/debug_path.sh builds and runs both compilers with quoted/backslash and newline paths, under the default C compiler and clang when available. The separate GDB-version wording finding D90 remains environment-specific.

    145. program_in retained every growing struct-definition prefix — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: sh tests/struct_defs_scale.sh

    Audit D15 found the self-hosted emitter appending each struct field to head, then prepending other generated declarations to that same string. That made the compiler's accumulator analysis miss the append loop, retaining quadratic intermediate copies (the 900-struct repro peaked above 4 GiB). Struct-definition fragments now collect separately and join once before head is assembled. The regression emits 900 structs with 20 fields each under a 1.5 GiB virtual-memory limit when the host supports it.

    146. Deep integer sums and interpolated strings overflowed compiler/C nesting — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: sh tests/expression_scale.sh

    Audit D16's flat 1,400-term integer sum recursed through the self-hosted checker/emitter and exhausted the compiler stack; D17's 256-term sum and 130-hole f-string exceeded clang's bracket limit. Simple-int + left spines now typecheck and emit iteratively, with the shared 2,000-node diagnostic retained for larger sums. Other deep left-associated binary spines are refused at 1,024 nodes, and recursive expression checking has a depth guard; the parser also pre-scans and refuses over-256 nested parentheses/calls or consecutive unary operators before recursive descent. Both emitters flatten f-string joins into a sequential statement-expression instead of nesting one block per piece. The generated tests pass 1,400 terms on both compilers, refuse 2,101-term sums and subtractions with located diagnostics, reject over-deep calls/unary chains, and compile the 256-term/130-hole clang cases.

    147. Typed tuple literals used synthesized element types in C — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: make -s test

    Audit D20's (float, u8) = (1, 2) passed typechecking but emitted a different C tuple struct: the self-hosted emitter chose the tuple type from synthesized (int, int) elements, despite checking each element under the destination component type. It now constructs the C tuple with the expected tuple type and emits integer-to-float literal adaptation. The fixture covers declarations, returns, function arguments, struct fields, array elements and Some payloads; both compilers produce the same values.

    148. The self-hosted emitter accepted pow and SoA loops in typechecking but had no lowering — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: make -s test

    Audit D25 exposed two phase-parity gaps: pow(x, y) was registered in the checker but fell through to an unsupported-call internal error, and the checker accepted for p in soa_value while the emitter only handled arrays and strings. The emitter now lowers pow to libm and SoA iteration through the per-element gather helper, preserving a stable length snapshot when the body mutates its source. The positive fixture runs on both compilers.

    149. F-string holes ignored trailing syntax and rejected surrounding whitespace — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: make -s test

    Audit D24 found that both compilers accepted only the first expression in a hole ({price:.2f} and {n, 99} silently printed the value), while the self-hosted re-lexer treated { n } as unexpected indentation. Hole text is now trimmed before lexing, the expression parser must consume the entire fragment, and both brace scanners skip quoted character literals as well as strings. The positive fixture covers whitespace and a } character literal; the two reject fixtures pin format-spec and trailing-expression refusal.

    150. Sized float conversions differed from the total wrapping contract — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: make -s test

    The self-hosted emitter sent to_u8..to_i64 from float/f32 through tycho_f2i, which traps outside the signed 64-bit range (and on NaN/inf). The native emitter already used tycho_f2u64, whose documented conversion truncates and wraps finite inputs modulo 2^64 and maps non-finite inputs to 0. The self-hosted lowering now uses the same helper. The fixture checks negative fractional wrap, an in-range signed conversion, to_u64(1e19), and NaN under both compilers.

    151. Equal float leaves in composite map keys hashed differently — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: make -s test

    Float equality treats 0.0 and -0.0 as equal, but hashing their IEEE-754 bit patterns differed. A map keyed by a struct (or nested struct) could therefore store two equal keys and fail to find an overwrite. Both emitters now canonicalize zero before hashing; runtime array-of-float key hashing uses the same helper. The nested-key fixture inserts +0.0 then -0.0 and verifies one entry with the updated value under both compilers.

    152. Self-hosted composite map-key validation admitted un-emittable leaves — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: make -s test

    The self-hosted key predicate treated every sized integer and f32 as a valid leaf in a composite map key, while the native compiler and supported hash emitter accept only the shared leaf set. A struct key with either field passed typechecking and then referenced an undefined generated hash function. The predicate now matches the native set and refuses these keys before emission.

    153. Large unsigned shift counts were misread as negative — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: make -s test

    Shift helpers took a signed long long count. Passing a u64 count at or above 2^63 converted it to a negative value, so the runtime aborted instead of applying the specified count >= width -> 0 rule. Both emitters now route counts through a helper that distinguishes the right operand's signedness before the width check; negative signed counts still abort. The shift-edge fixture covers both left and right shifts with a u64 count of 2^63, while the abort fixture keeps the negative signed-count diagnostic intact.

    154. Synthesized locals could shadow user bindings — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: make -s test

    Both front-ends emitted internal locals in the same identifier namespace as source bindings. A user _fc0, _cx0, _ms36_0, or _mx36_0_0 could therefore be shadowed by a for-in or assignment temporary, silently changing results. Synthetic binding names now use Ztycho_; the lexers reject that prefix in source, and resolution permits it only for the internally synthesized AST bindings. The multi-assignment fixture pins the exact _mx36_0_0/_ms36_0 collision shape, the for-in fixture covers _fc0/_fi0, and the reject fixture defines the reserved namespace boundary.

    155. Resolver recursion depth leaked across recovered diagnostics — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: sh tests/depth_recovery.sh

    The native resolver uses longjmp to recover from a bad statement and continue collecting diagnostics. When an error unwound nested resolve_exp calls, their normal depth decrements were skipped; after 2000 ordinary errors every later statement was misreported as too deeply nested. Recovery boundaries now reset the counter. The generated check sends 2105 independent unresolved-name errors through both compilers and verifies there are no fabricated depth errors.

    156. Enum variant spellings leaked into generated C identifiers — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: make -s test

    Payload variants were emitted as C union members and E_<enum>_<variant> payload typedefs. C keywords (default, case, while), standard macros (EOF, NULL), and a user enum named Shape_Circle could therefore break the C build. Both emitters now use prefixed C-only union fields (V_<name>) and payload types (TychoPayload_<enum>_<variant>), while diagnostics and str() keep the source spelling. The fixture exercises all three collision classes.

    157. Deep non-addition binary spines could exhaust the self-hosted typechecker — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: sh tests/expression_scale.sh

    The flat-integer-add path was bounded and iterative, but other left-associated binary operators still recursed through the entire AST; a 2101-term subtraction stack-overflowed tychoc1 instead of returning a diagnostic. The self-hosted typechecker now guards recursive expression walks, and the native resolver checks non-+ left spines iteratively before descending. Both reject the deep subtraction with a located depth diagnostic, while 1400-term + remains accepted and 2101-term + retains its specific max-2000 diagnostic.

    158. Double underscores in source type names were mistaken for compiler mangling — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: make -s test

    The self-hosted variant ownership check split every type name at its first __, so a main-package enum named Shape__Kind was misclassified as belonging to a package. Generic instance names also used Box__int without checking for a source struct of that exact name, producing duplicate C definitions. Ownership now uses the recorded package prefix, and colliding generated instance names receive a deterministic _2 suffix (matching the native compiler). The fixture covers enum matching and the generic/source-name collision.

    159. Large constant array literals gave gcc one store per element to optimize — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: sh tests/array_literal_scale.sh

    A 15,625-element constant table lowered to 15,625 assignments inside a GNU statement expression; gcc -O3 spent most of its time optimizing that giant basic block. Both emitters now recognize large (64+) scalar-literal arrays, write a block-scope static const initializer, allocate the destination in its arena, and memcpy the constant bytes once. Nonconstant and heap-element arrays keep the element-wise path and its ownership copies. The generated test compiles and runs the 15,625-element case under -O3 with both compilers.

    160. F-string C joins and lambda scope snapshots grew quadratically — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: sh tests/emitter_memory_scale.sh

    _fchain repeatedly concatenated the whole emitted prefix for every piece, and _lambda copied each enclosing local table before compiling each closure. The long f-string now joins an array of C fragments once; lambda bindings are appended and removed with the existing mark/restore discipline instead of copying the outer tables. A bounded generated check emits 4,000 f-string holes and 8,000 lambdas each capturing a local below a 1.2-GiB address-space limit.

    161. Packed fields passed to inout were dereferenced at natural alignment — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: make -s test Pinned-by: sh tests/packed_inout_ffi.sh

    Passing &packed.field as T * lets the callee dereference an address that need not satisfy alignof(T). Both emitters now compute the field's byte address from its packed aggregate plus offsetof, copy into an aligned local with memcpy, call through that local, and copy the result back. The same lowering covers array elements, user-defined subscript projections, and extern calls. The fixture runs all three place forms under AddressSanitizer/UBSan; tests/packed_inout_ffi.sh also covers an extern call under UBSan and -Werror=address-of-packed-member.

    162. The debug gate accepted only one GDB spelling for a missing line — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: make -s debug-check

    GDB 15.1 reports No line 999 in file, while GDB 17 reports No compiled code for line 999; the gate had required only the latter. tools/tycho-debug/run.sh now accepts either version-specific message. make debug-check passes all seven legs on Fedora's GDB 17.2, including the previously load-sensitive Ctrl-C leg. The Ubuntu 24.04/GDB 15.1 binary is unavailable on this host, but its reported message is explicitly covered by the matcher.

    163. Native generic mismatch diagnostics exposed package-mangled names — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: sh tests/package_diagnostic_name.sh

    When a package-qualified generic call failed parameter-pattern matching, the native compiler printed its internal symbol (math__clamp) while the self-hosted compiler printed the source name (clamp). The native diagnostic now uses nominal_name() like its sibling paths. A focused script checks that both compilers reject the mismatched call without exposing an internal mangled symbol (the native diagnostic retains the package qualifier).

    164. C-string shims silently discarded bytes after an embedded NUL — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: make -s corelib

    The remaining crypto-hex and SQLite path/SQL inputs now reject interior NULs before crossing a C-string ABI. Conversely, output APIs that are byte streams (io.read_line, os.run, and os.exec_out) now cross as length-carrying bytes and convert to Tycho strings only after the explicit length is known. The corelib goldens cover binary-safe line/fold reads and child output containing A\0B, alongside the crypto and SQLite rejection cases.

    165. Compression truncated zlib lengths and under-enforced its output ceiling — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: make -s corelib Pinned-by: sh tests/compress_limits.sh

    The gzip and raw inflate paths now clamp growth to the actual output ceiling, probe one byte beyond it so an exact-limit stream can finish its trailer, and reject trailing non-member data while supporting concatenated gzip members. All four zlib entry points refuse lengths that cannot fit uInt; compressor output bounds are checked before narrowing avail_out. The reduced-ceiling C gate proves the inclusive 65,536-byte boundary, the first byte over it, and oversized lengths without allocating multi-gigabyte buffers.

    166. POSIX timezone offsets accumulated unbounded decimal fields — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: make -s corelib

    offset_at now parses offset and transition components with digit-by-digit bounds, limits zone offsets to 24 hours and transition times to POSIX's extended 167-hour range, and validates month/week/day and Julian rule ranges before casting. The datetime golden includes oversized hours, minutes, seconds, and a 20-digit offset that previously returned a nonsensical positive value.

    167. Handle destructors could return aggregate values that require hidden out-parameters — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: make -s test

    The FFI declaration check now refuses bytes, [int], or [float] destructor returns in both compilers: scope-exit and explicit close calls pass only the handle, whereas those C ABIs require two additional output parameters. New reject fixtures pin both the byte and scalar-array cases.

    168. A checkpoint could truncate the only committed store before its replacement was durable — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: make -s db-check

    core:io.rename now provides same-filesystem atomic replacement. store.flush writes a sibling temporary file, syncs it, and renames it over the old store; wal.checkpoint then syncs the new file and parent directory before rewinding the WAL. Replacement preserves the existing regular file's mode bits on POSIX and its ACL on Windows, rather than inheriting the temp file's permissions. The database gate checks 0640 and 0600 survive replacement, then forces a partial temp write with a 512-byte file-size limit, checks the old store is byte-identical, and reopens it with all rows.

    169. A mutation remained visible after its WAL append was refused — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: make -s db-check

    exec._create and exec._insert now validate without mutation, append the WAL record, and only then apply it. The NotWritten leg follows a refused CREATE with SELECT and INSERT and verifies neither can observe a table that was never logged.

    170. Separate tycho-db processes could overwrite one another's commits — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: make -s db-check

    core:io.lock_exclusive uses a nonblocking process lock (flock on POSIX, LockFileEx on Windows); wal.open_db acquires <store>.lock before reading store or log state. The OS releases it on process exit, including a crash. The database gate attempts a local CLI write while the server owns the same store and requires a database is locked refusal before any output.

    171. Store decoding trusted row shape and integer encodings — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: make -s db-check

    The decoder now checks row arity and value/column types before rebuilding an index, refuses trailing bytes, unknown column-tag bits, truncated values, positive magnitudes outside int64, and noncanonical negative zero. Signed magnitude encoding handles INT_MIN without negating it. Six corrupted-store fixtures must fail as Corrupt with empty stdout rather than panic or accept malformed data.

    172. A corrupt store plus an empty WAL could be rebuilt and overwritten — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: make -s db-check

    wal.open_db no longer treats a zero-length log as evidence that a corrupt store can be reconstructed from the beginning. The gate checks that opening such a pair fails without answering or changing the damaged store.

    173. A silent tycho-db client could monopolize the serial server — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: make -s db-check

    Server reads now have a one-second absolute line deadline (including slow-drip input), and a session is capped at 60 seconds. net.Timeout is reported as an idle timeout and the connection is closed before the next is accepted. The gate keeps an idle TCP connection open while a second real client must complete.

    174. An indexed cross-type comparison returned no rows instead of an error — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: make -s db-check

    Before opening an IndexEq path, exec checks the literal type against the indexed column. SELECT and EXPLAIN both reject INT KEY = text with the same Uncomparable diagnostic as the scan path; direct regression legs prevent an empty index result from hiding the type error.

    175. Sorted-array KEY index maintenance copied every shifted entry — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: make -s db-check

    The sorted vector was replaced with a hash index from a type-tagged string key to insertion-ordered row positions. Inserts and index rebuilds are amortized linear overall instead of repeatedly shifting/deep-copying prior entries. A 20,000-row reverse-order insert plus flush/reopen/probe runs under a 192-MiB address-space limit in tests/fixtures/tycho_db_index_scale.src.

    176. recv(ch) returned a payload allocated in the callee's freed scope — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: make -s test Pinned-by: sh tests/recv_loop_scale.sh

    The self-hosted emitter's receive lowering now passes cs.arena both to the channel-copy helper and to the Some allocation. A receive used as a direct return, after a local bind, and through or_else is stressed with arena churn in tests/recv_escape.ty; both compilers preserve the 16-KiB payload.

    177. bytes and scoped string accumulators bypassed self-hosted in-place append — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: make -s test Pinned-by: sh tests/accumulator_scope.sh

    Accumulator discovery/emission now covers bytes plus match/select and related nested scopes. The 50,000-iteration fixture runs under both compilers and checks the exact output; the dedicated gate applies a 1.2-GiB process limit so a return to repeated whole-value concatenation is caught as a scale regression.

    178. Several untrusted sizes reached allocation or narrowing operations unchecked — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: make -s test Pinned-by: sh tests/audit_runtime_guards.sh Pinned-by: sh tests/http_header_guard.sh

    Array reserve checks negative and excessive capacities before its no-op fast path, and composite-array multiplication is checked before allocation. Map reserve rejects negative/over-index-range capacities before doubling or rounding its entry/index sizes; focused fast- and composite-map cases exercise both compilers. Channel-cell sizing checks multiplication and each power-of-two doubling before allocation. Regex group indices are bounded before capture-vector allocation, and subjects are capped at 4,096 bytes; the runtime gate executes a failing a+b search at that inclusive limit under a 10-second timeout, then checks that over-limit subjects are refused. The HTTP client refuses control characters and values too long for its serialized Content-Type header; the net shim refuses descriptors that do not fit its OS int descriptor type. Runtime and header gates exercise these guards under both compilers.

    179. TLS timeout wrappers existed only as package-private helpers — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: make -s corelib

    tls.connect_timeout and tls.set_timeout_ms are public API and recorded in surface.lock. Both reject millisecond values outside signed 32-bit range; the Windows connect path now uses nonblocking connect plus a bounded wait. Targeted TLS tests pass on this POSIX host; the Windows path remains unvalidated on a Windows host because neither one nor Wine is available here.

    180. HTTP parsing conflated absent fields, malformed framing and malformed request grammar — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: make -s server-check

    httpd.has_header distinguishes an absent field from a present-but-empty one; framing rejects any Transfer-Encoding and empty Content-Length. The parser now rejects invalid field names/values, obsolete folding, extra request-line tokens and unsupported versions. Range positions ignore leading zeroes when enforcing the digit limit. Empty/duplicate headers and malformed request lines are pinned by the server gate. (The separate 413-vs-431 status distinction remains open.)

    181. The FFI integer table and SQLite example contradicted the compiler — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: make -s docs-fences Pinned-by: make -s corelib-doc-check

    The FFI reference now distinguishes C int (i32) from Tycho int (signed 64-bit), and describes to_i32 as a narrowing conversion. The SQLite example now uses the actual sized FFI types and builds with both compilers; stale NUL, append, path and corelib boundary descriptions were corrected as well.

    182. TOML table placement discarded implicit parents and array-of-table children — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: make -s corelib

    The TOML placer now merges a later explicit parent table with the implicit table created by an earlier child header, and nested headers descend into the latest array-of-tables element. The corelib fixture asserts that parent and child keys both survive and that subtables attach to the right array element.

    183. TOML parsing had unbounded recursion and quadratic table reconstruction — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: make -s corelib

    The parser now refuses documents over 64 KiB, more than 512 assignments/headers, or nesting beyond 128 levels (including dotted key/header paths). These bounds cap the worst-case cost of the current insertion-ordered table representation; fixtures cover deep values, deep headers, excessive entry counts and oversized input. Valid smaller TOML remains parsed as before.

    184. ZIP creation truncated names and counts into classic-format fields — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: make -s corelib Pinned-by: make -s snap-check

    zip.create_checked rejects entry counts, name lengths, data sizes and offsets that do not fit the classic ZIP fields; the old create entry point is now a fail-fast wrapper instead of silently forging an archive. The corelib fixture checks both the 65,536-byte name and 65,536-entry boundaries, and tycho-snap uses the checked API.

    185. TOML dotted keys split inside quotes and malformed strings became valid values — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: make -s corelib

    A quote-aware dotted-key scanner is shared by assignments and table headers; basic quoted keys decode escapes, duplicate headers compare canonical keys, and bare keys are syntax-checked. Literal strings with trailing text and \\u surrogates now return Err. Corelib fixtures cover quoted dots/escapes, quoted headers, canonical duplicates, trailing literal text and surrogate escapes.

    186. JSON object parsing rescanned all prior keys for every member — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: make -s corelib

    _parse_object now keeps a per-object hash map from key to its original slot. Duplicate members still replace the value at the first key's position, while unique-member lookup no longer scans the growing key vector. The corelib test parses and probes a 20,000-member object.

    187. Markdown rescanned the same found ] for every unmatched [ — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: make -s corelib

    Inline rendering now caches the found ] and ) positions and reuses them while subsequent link/image openers precede those terminators. A closing delimiter that fails to form a link no longer forces every following [ to rescan to the same byte. Corelib output checks 20,000 [ plus ] and 20,000 ![ plus ] shapes remain byte-for-byte literal.

    188. ZIP extraction rescanned the central directory and unbounded file suffix — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: make -s corelib Pinned-by: make -s snap-check

    Each zip.Meta now carries its local-header offset, and extract_entry(bytes, meta) consumes it directly. Iterating one list() result avoids a full directory walk per entry. EOCD search is restricted to the ZIP-legal 65,557-byte suffix and requires the comment to end at EOF; the malformed trailing-junk test is rejected.

    189. Option reference claimed a bare None comparison was valid — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: make -s parse-check Pinned-by: make -s docs-fences

    The reference now agrees with the normative rule: a bare None has no expected type in ==/!=; use a typed value or is None. The self-hosted typechecker rejects ungrounded Option/Result operands with a located error instead of accepting them for a later location-less emitter failure. Three reject fixtures cover Some(1) == None, opt == None and bare Ok/Err comparison.

    190. Self-hosted builtin-collision advice mislabeled subpackages as main — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: make -s test

    Builtin-shadow warnings now use the declaring package prefix captured during global symbol registration, not the resolver's later current-file state. The warning distinguishes package-local calls from external qualified calls. A subpackage len fixture checks the advice.

    191. Self-hosted emitter could not construct generic enum variants through a package qualifier — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: make -s test

    Qualified calls now search generic enum templates only in the declaring package, then infer type parameters from payloads or apply explicit type arguments before emitting the specialized variant. tests/pkg/generic_enum_variant_cross checks both box.Has(41) and box.Nothing$(int) under both compilers.

    192. Self-hosted loops retained overwritten strings and variadic packs in _scope — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: make -s test Pinned-by: sh tests/loop_temp_recycle.sh

    [string] element stores now evaluate into the iteration scratch and call the runtime's copy-and-recycle setter. Variadic packs and their transient arguments also use the current loop scratch (_curown) instead of the function arena. A two-million-iteration overwrite plus variadic-call test runs under a 96-MiB virtual-memory limit for both compilers.

    193. Replacing a heap value in a long-lived map abandoned its owned graph — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: make -s test Pinned-by: sh tests/map_overwrite_scale.sh

    Both emitters now copy map replacements into the owner before recursively recycling the evicted value. Generated typed recyclers cover nested arrays, composite maps, structs, enums, tuples, options/results, struct-of-arrays and captured closure environments; composite-key and value allocations are reclaimed on delete as well. String leaves use the runtime's owner-checked recycler. The 96-MiB regression exercises 200,000 string and nested-array replacements plus 100,000 struct, enum and captured-closure replacements, then deletes an enum value, under both compilers.

    194. HTTP request-budget overflow mapped a declared oversized body to 431 — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: make -s corelib Pinned-by: make -s server-check

    ReqErr.BodyTooLarge is now distinct from an unterminated oversized header (TooLarge). The server and tycho-kvsrv map framed body overflow to 413, while header overflow remains 431; reason_phrase(413) and the error-page explanation are present. Corelib's reduced-cap socket test and the server's wire test pin the split.

    195. resolve_under treated a dangling symlink as an ordinary missing leaf — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: make -s server-check

    When realpath fails, resolve_under now checks whether the current path is itself a symlink (lstat on POSIX, reparse metadata on Windows) before walking to its parent. A dangling link is refused; an ordinary absent file under a resolved in-root ancestor still returns its lexical path for a 404. The server gate covers both outcomes.

    196. Package suggestions exposed names that the next compile rejects as private — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: make -s test

    Both package-symbol suggestion paths now omit _-prefixed functions and externs from edit-distance and prefix-related candidates. A package reject fixture with only lib._helper verifies that lib.helper(...) gets no unusable suggestion.

    197. Compiler CLIs accepted empty output names, swallowed linker paths and indexed invalid programs — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: make -s test Pinned-by: sh tests/cli_flags.sh

    --symbols now refuses both resolver and typechecker errors, and prints no partial index for ill-typed input. Both compilers explicitly reject -o "" before either a binary build or --emit-c, without leaving a stray .c file. Separated -L/-I require a non-option directory argument instead of consuming the next flag. The CLI regression checks these refusals under both compilers and both output modes.

    198. Native procedure-signature lookup scaled quadratically — FIXED 2026-09-29

    Pinned-by: sh tests/proc_signature_scale.sh Pinned-by: sh tests/name_registry_scale.sh

    sig_find and sig_find_quiet use an append-aware open-addressed index that preserves first-match behavior. The companion D31 fix in #201 indexes constant, variant and generic registries and caches source line starts for warning snippets. The 40,000-procedure chain and focused remaining-registry stress test both pass.

    199. Same-arena copies from replaceable string slots aliased recycled buffers — FIXED 2026-09-27

    Pinned-by: make -s test Pinned-by: sh tests/run.sh --one tests/elem_recycle.ty

    The self-hosted emitter treated a string read from an array/field slot as safe to share merely because source and destination used the same arena. Replacing the slot can recycle that buffer, corrupting the copied value. _shares now forces a copy for fields, tuple fields and indexed places; elem_recycle covers overwrite, self-assignment and a surviving snapshot.

    200. Nested generic calls and constructor payloads repeated subtree work — FIXED 2026-09-29

    Pinned-by: sh tests/emitter_nested_calls.sh

    The self-hosted typechecker now reuses a UFCS receiver type during generic inference, and the emitter's argument-type path infers call types without re-emitting their arguments. The native resolver likewise reuses concrete payload types after generic struct/enum inference, while still resolving partial None/Ok/Err values against their expected type. The regression compiles 64-deep generic method chains and 32-deep nested generic struct/enum constructors under both compilers, with a 1.2-GiB memory cap and a 20-second compile limit.

    201. Native constant, variant, generic and warning-location scans scaled with all prior input — FIXED 2026-09-29

    Pinned-by: sh tests/name_registry_scale.sh Pinned-by: sh tests/proc_signature_scale.sh

    The native compiler now uses first-match-preserving open-addressed indexes for constants, variants and generic procedures, alongside the procedure-signature index from #198. Both compiler pipelines pre-index source lines once instead of rescanning the source for every warning excerpt. The self-hosted declaration registration uses stable O(n log n) source-order sorting instead of selection sort. Stress coverage registers 12,000 constants and generics, 1,500 variants, and emits 6,000 warnings under both compilers; this lane and the 40,000-procedure chain pass.

    202. Native codegen refused deeply nested loops that the self-hosted compiler accepted — FIXED 2026-09-30

    Pinned-by: sh tests/nested_blocks_scale.sh

    The D03 verifier found that native codegen's loop and arena-unwind stacks were hard-capped at 64, while tychoc1 accepted deeper nesting. Those stacks now hold 256 entries, matching the parser's nesting ceiling. A 125-level nested-loop program compiles and runs under both compilers with a 1.2-GiB cap. Extremely deep nesting remains intentionally bounded; the 250-level self-hosted compile's measured memory cost is not claimed to be constant.

    203. Package-local builtin names disagreed between compilers and produced false collision advice — FIXED 2026-09-30

    Pinned-by: sh tests/package_builtin_shadow.sh

    A bare call inside a package now resolves that package's own declaration before falling back to a builtin, matching native resolution. Package-collision advice states that local calls select the package function and external calls should be qualified. The regression defines lib.pow, checks the warning and verifies the result is 3.0 under both compilers.
